Context Narrative Diffs
BMS-3768 PR #503 View on GitHub ↗

feat(wms): complete internal transfer alerts — CTC + hot transfer + ack queue

Jira: Review Story wms gulf phase-1-crawl bk (Big Kahuna) polished fast-trackable
Gulf warehouses had no structured way to flag urgent inbound transfers — close-to-code (CTC) product arrived unannounced, and receiving teams only discovered the problem at the dock, leading to disputed write-offs and wasted labor. This story adds two alert types (CTC on dispatch, Hot Transfer on manual flag), an acknowledge flow, and two record-page LWCs so receiving staff get advance warning and can triage by urgency.
Acceptance criteria (Gherkin, 5 scenarios)
Scenario: Close-to-code transfer triggers alert to receiving warehouse Given an internal transfer is created ... containing SKUs where any line item's code date is within the configured alert threshold (e.g., ≤14 days from today) When the transfer record is saved with Status = 'Submitted' Then A Transfer_Alert__c record is created with Alert_Type__c = 'Close to Code' ... And A platform notification is sent to users in the receiving Warehouse Receiving permission group And The transfer record displays a visible warning badge ... Scenario: Hot transfer flagged manually by sending warehouse Given a warehouse manager ... creating an internal transfer that requires expedited handling When the manager toggles the 'Hot Transfer' flag and selects a priority reason Then A Transfer_Alert__c record is created with Alert_Type__c = 'Hot Transfer' ... And Receiving users get a platform notification with the priority reason And The transfer appears with a red priority indicator, sorted above non-hot transfers Scenario: Receiving team acknowledges transfer alert When the receiving lead clicks 'Acknowledge' on a close-to-code alert Then The alert status changes from 'Pending' to 'Acknowledged' with user + timestamp And Unacknowledged alerts older than the escalation window remain highlighted as 'Overdue' Scenario: Transfer contains mix of close-to-code and normal product Then The alert references only the flagged line items, not the whole transfer And The transfer header shows 'Partial Close to Code — 3 of 12 items' Scenario: Alert threshold is configurable per warehouse or brand Then Each flagged SKU is evaluated against its applicable threshold band

What Was Built

This PR delivers the full internal-transfer-alerts feature end to end: a new Transfer_Alert__c object keyed off the receiving warehouse's existing CTC_Threshold__mdt urgency bands, a synchronous (non-platform-event) upsert in TransferGroupTriggerService that fires on the New→In Progress dispatch edge (CTC) and on the Is_Hot_Transfer__c false→true toggle (Hot Transfer), a DTO layer so the two new record-page LWCs (transferAlertBanner, transferAlertQueue) work identically in namespaced and no-namespace orgs, and an acknowledge flow with idempotent upsert-on-External_Id__c semantics throughout. The branch also carries a long tail of no-namespace/CI compatibility fixes (rule-7 test prefixes, Type.forName fallback, CMDT IN-clause queries) required to keep the nightly snapshot build green.

Is_Hot_Transfer__c (the manual flag), Hot_Transfer_Priority_Reason__c (picklist: Promotion/Emergency Stock/Customer Commitment/Other), Hot_Transfer_Notes__c — the three fields the sending warehouse manager sets to trigger a Hot Transfer alert.
Apex — Services, Executables, DTOs & Trigger 20 files
TransferGroupTriggerService.cls +197
Adds publishTransferAlerts (CTC, gated on the New→In Progress edge) and publishHotTransferAlerts (gated on the false→true toggle). Both upsert directly on External_Id__c in SYSTEM_MODE and swallow/log any exception so alerting can never block the underlying save — see Key Decisions.

Sends the platform custom notification to holders of the WMS_Transfer_Alert_Receiver custom permission — the notify half of both alert paths.

TransferAlertDTO.cls + TransferAlert.cls +105
Global camelCase DTO plus a sibling non-global factory (TransferAlert.fromSObject) — split per ADR-0007 rule 5 so the mapping can evolve without freezing the managed-package API. Required because @AuraEnabled responses serialize with the ohfy__ prefix in managed orgs, which breaks bare field access in LWC.

Includes the bulk (2+ group) coverage and the hot-alert-suppressed-when-blank-location coverage added in the review-fix round.

Key Decisions

  1. Direct synchronous upsert instead of a platform event. The PR description and Jira history describe a platform-event pattern (Transfer_Alert_Event__e + a TransferAlertEventHandler subscriber). Commit 33eea25e9 ("remove Transfer_Alert_Event__e to stay under DE platform-event cap") shows this was later ripped out — the Developer Edition scratch-org pool has a platform-event object-count cap, and this feature would have pushed over it. The shipped code upserts Transfer_Alert__c directly inside TransferGroupTriggerService, synchronously, in the same transaction as the dispatch/toggle. See Open Items — this makes several review comments about that class moot without anyone updating the text that references it.
  2. Alerts are advisory, never blocking. Both publishTransferAlerts and publishHotTransferAlerts wrap their entire body in try/catch, logging and swallowing on any failure — a broken alert must never fail the underlying transfer dispatch or hot-flag toggle.
  3. Factory logic split from the global DTO (ADR-0007 rule 5). TransferAlertDTO stays a thin, frozen global shape; TransferAlert.fromSObject() lives in a sibling non-global class so the mapping logic can change later without breaking the managed-package API contract.
  4. Re-query by External_Id__c, never by in-memory Id, after an upsert. Database.upsert does not back-fill Id on the UPDATE leg of an external-ID match — both alert paths re-query by External_Id__c before notifying, specifically to survive re-toggles/redelivery-style re-runs.
  5. Apex-side severity re-sort. Urgency_Band__c sorts alphabetically in SOQL ("Act" < "Critical" < "Monitor") but Critical must outrank Act — E_TransferAlerts.sortBySeverity() re-orders in Apex using an explicit rank map after the query, rather than relying on client-side LWC re-sorting alone.

Open Items

Stale PR description — the PR body's "Key fixes from code review" section references TransferAlertEventHandler.queryForNotification and platform-event redelivery handling. That class does not exist anywhere in this diff, on main, or anywhere in the branch history I could grep for (git grep queryForNotification returns nothing on either ref). The architecture was refactored to a direct synchronous upsert in TransferGroupTriggerService (see Key Decisions #1) after that class was written, and the description text was never updated to match. The original review round's BLOCKER/WARNING findings against it are effectively moot on the shipped code, but a reader who trusts the PR body without checking the diff would come away with the wrong mental model of how alerts fire.
Fixed — [BLOCKER] hardcoded ohfy. namespace prefix on 6 cross-package call sites in WMS_UI_Wrappers.cls, which would fail Apex compile in the no-namespace nightly snapshot org. Verified: the shipped diff calls E_TransferAlerts.* / TransferAlert.fromSObject unqualified, matching every other cross-package call in that file.
Fixed — both [WARNING]s on the (now-removed) event-handler redelivery path are moot per the architecture change above, but the equivalent concern was addressed in the new code: both trigger-service methods re-query by External_Id__c (not Id) before notifying, and both wrap their body in try/catch so a query/notify failure can't propagate.
Fixed — [WARNING] silent skip when a Hot Transfer is toggled with New_Location__c still blank. The shipped code adds an explicit else if branch with a Logger.getInstance().error(...) call, plus a new test (TransferGroupTriggerService_T) asserting no alert is created and the branch fires — closing the exact gap the review-round SUGGESTION asked for.
Fixed — both [SUGGESTION]s on E_TransferAlerts.cls: severity sort now runs in Apex (sortBySeverity / URGENCY_RANK) instead of relying only on client-side LWC re-sort, and getTransferLineCount now uses a COUNT(Id) aggregate instead of materializing the full line list.
Fixed — [SUGGESTION] bulk-safety test gap — S_TransferAlerts_T now has bulkBuildAlertsTwoGroupsReturnsBothAlerts exercising 2+ dispatched groups in one call.
Fixed — reviewer's scope objection to an unrelated .github/workflows/ci.yml retry-logic change. That file does not appear anywhere in the final diff — the commit log shows it was dropped (chore: remove out-of-scope work accidentally bundled into this PR, plus two follow-up fix(ci): commits), consistent with the reviewer's request to revert it.
Still open (advisory, per Jira comment history) — banner's wiredAlerts shows the same empty state on a wire error as on legitimately-zero alerts — no visual distinction. Called out as "acceptable for v1" by the reviewer, not blocking.
Still open (advisory) — PILL_BASE / daysToCodeLabel are duplicated between transferAlertBanner and transferAlertQueue. Flagged as a good candidate for a shared utility if a third alert surface is ever added — not urgent at 2 consumers.

2542 @AuraEnabled(cacheable=true)
2543 public static List<TransferAlertDTO> getTransferAlerts(Id transferGroupId) {
2544 try {
2545 List<Transfer_Alert__c> alerts = E_TransferAlerts.getAlertsForTransferGroup(
2546 transferGroupId
2547 );
2548 Integer lineCount = E_TransferAlerts.getTransferLineCount(transferGroupId);
2549 List<TransferAlertDTO> dtos = new List<TransferAlertDTO>();
2550 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : alerts) {
2551 TransferAlertDTO dto = TransferAlert.fromSObject(a);
2552 dto.totalTransferLineCount = lineCount;
2553 dtos.add(dto);
2554 }
2555 return dtos;
2556 } catch (Exception e) {
2557 Logger.getInstance().error('WMS_UI_Wrappers.getTransferAlerts failed', e);
2558 Logger.getInstance().flush();
2559 throw e;
2560 }
2561 }

2563 // Not cacheable: acknowledge mutates these alerts and the imperative caller in
2564 // transferAlertQueue has no refreshApex handle (same pattern as line 1336).
2565 @AuraEnabled
2566 public static List<TransferAlertDTO> getWarehouseInboundAlerts(Id locationId) {
2567 try {
2568 List<Transfer_Alert__c> alerts = E_TransferAlerts.getInboundAlertsForWarehouse(
2569 locationId
2570 );
2571 List<TransferAlertDTO> dtos = new List<TransferAlertDTO>();
2572 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : alerts) {
2573 TransferAlertDTO dto = TransferAlert.fromSObject(a);
2574 // getInboundAlertsForWarehouse queries Transfer_Group__r.Name; set it here
2575 // since fromSObject omits it to avoid SObjectException when not queried.
2576 dto.groupName = a.Transfer_Group__r != null ? a.Transfer_Group__r.Name : null;
2577 dtos.add(dto);
2578 }
2579 return dtos;
2580 } catch (Exception e) {
2581 Logger.getInstance().error('WMS_UI_Wrappers.getWarehouseInboundAlerts failed', e);
2582 Logger.getInstance().flush();
2583 throw e;
2584 }

1424 New_Location__c = dest.Id,
1425 Status__c = 'New',

1427 Is_External__c = false,
1428 Transfer_Date__c = Date.today()
1429 );

1432 Transfer_Group__c = grp.Id,
1433 Receiving_Location__c = dest.Id,
1434 Origin_Location__c = origin.Id,
1435 Alert_Type__c = 'Close to Code',
1436 Urgency_Band__c = 'Critical',
1437 Status__c = 'New',
1438 Days_To_Code__c = 5,
1439 Case_Quantity__c = 10,
1440 Detected_At__c = Datetime.now()
1441 );
1442 insert alert;
1443 return alert;

1import { LightningElement, api, track, wire } from "lwc";
2import { loadStyle } from "lightning/platformResourceLoader";
3import { ShowToastEvent } from "lightning/platformShowToastEvent";
4import formFactor from "@salesforce/client/formFactor";
5import tailwindCSS from "@salesforce/resourceUrl/tailwindCSS_wms";
6import getTransferAlerts from "@salesforce/apex/WMS_UI_Wrappers.getTransferAlerts";
7import acknowledgeTransferAlert from "@salesforce/apex/WMS_UI_Wrappers.acknowledgeTransferAlert";
8import { refreshApex } from "@salesforce/apex";
9
10const BAND_STYLES = {
11 Critical:
12 "background: rgb(var(--ohfy-danger, 163 45 45)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface, 255 255 255));",
13 Act: "background: rgb(var(--ohfy-accent, 228 242 35)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-primary, 0 0 0));",
14 Monitor:
15 "background: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface-muted, 244 242 240)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-text-muted, 84 84 84));",
16};
17const BAND_HEADER = {
18 Critical: "background: rgb(var(--ohfy-danger, 163 45 45) / 0.08);",
19 Act: "background: rgb(var(--ohfy-accent, 228 242 35) / 0.2);",
20 Monitor: "",
21};
22const HOT_HEADER = "background: rgb(var(--ohfy-danger, 163 45 45) / 0.08);";
23const PILL_BASE =
24 "display: inline-block; font-size: 0.6rem; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.04rem; padding: 0.1rem 0.45rem; border-radius: 0.75rem;";
25
26export default class TransferAlertBanner extends LightningElement {
27 @api recordId;
28
29 @track _alerts = [];
30 @track _acknowledging = new Set();
31 @track isLoading = true;
32 _wiredResult;
33
34 get isMobile() {
35 return formFactor === "Small";
36 }
37
38 connectedCallback() {
39 Promise.all([loadStyle(this, tailwindCSS)]);
40 }
41
42 @wire(getTransferAlerts, { transferGroupId: "$recordId" })
43 wiredAlerts(result) {
44 this._wiredResult = result;
45 if (result.data !== undefined || result.error) {
46 this.isLoading = false;
47 this._alerts = result.data || [];
48 }
49 }
50
51 get isEmpty() {
52 return !this.isLoading && (!this._alerts || this._alerts.length === 0);
53 }
54
55 get enrichedAlerts() {
56 return this._alerts.map(a => {
57 const isHot = a.alertType === "Hot Transfer";
58 const isAcknowledged = a.status === "Acknowledged";
59 const band = a.urgencyBand || "Monitor";
60
61 const headerStyle = isHot ? HOT_HEADER : BAND_HEADER[band] || "";
62
63 const typePillStyle =
64 PILL_BASE +
65 (isHot
66 ? " background: rgb(var(--ohfy-danger, 163 45 45)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface, 255 255 255));"
67 : " background: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface-muted, 244 242 240)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-text-muted, 84 84 84));");
68
69 const bandPillStyle = PILL_BASE + " " + (BAND_STYLES[band] || BAND_STYLES.Monitor);
70
71 let daysToCodeLabel = null;
72 if (!isHot && a.daysToCode != null) {
73 const d = a.daysToCode;
74 daysToCodeLabel = d < 0 ? `${Math.abs(d)} day(s) past code` : `${d} day(s) to code`;
75 }
76
77 let acknowledgedAtFormatted = null;
78 if (a.acknowledgedAt) {
79 acknowledgedAtFormatted = new Date(a.acknowledgedAt).toLocaleString();
80 }
81
82 return {
83 ...a,
84 isHot,
85 isAcknowledged,
86 showBand: !isHot && !!a.urgencyBand,
87 headerStyle,
88 typePillStyle,
89 bandPillStyle,
90 daysToCodeLabel,
91 showDaysToCode: !!daysToCodeLabel,
92 isAcknowledging: this._acknowledging.has(a.recordId),
93 acknowledgedAtFormatted,
94 };
95 });
96 }
97
98 get showPartialLabel() {
99 if (this._alerts.length === 0) return false;
100 const ctcAlerts = this._alerts.filter(a => a.alertType === "Close to Code");
101 if (ctcAlerts.length === 0) return false;
102 const ctcItemCount = new Set(ctcAlerts.map(a => a.itemId).filter(Boolean)).size;
103 const total = this._alerts[0].totalTransferLineCount;
104 if (total != null) {
105 return ctcItemCount < total;
106 }
107 // Fallback when count is unavailable: partial if not all alerted items are CTC
108 return ctcItemCount < new Set(this._alerts.map(a => a.itemId).filter(Boolean)).size;
109 }
110
111 get alertedItemCount() {
112 return new Set(
113 this._alerts
114 .filter(a => a.alertType === "Close to Code")
115 .map(a => a.itemId)
116 .filter(Boolean)
117 ).size;
118 }
119
120 get totalLineCount() {
121 const total = this._alerts.length > 0 ? this._alerts[0].totalTransferLineCount : null;
122 return total != null
123 ? total
124 : new Set(this._alerts.map(a => a.itemId).filter(Boolean)).size;
125 }
126
127 async handleAcknowledge(event) {
128 const alertId = event.currentTarget.dataset.id;
129 if (!alertId || this._acknowledging.has(alertId)) return;
130
131 this._acknowledging = new Set([...this._acknowledging, alertId]);
132 try {
133 await acknowledgeTransferAlert({ alertId });
134 try {
135 await refreshApex(this._wiredResult);
136 } catch (refreshError) {
137 // The acknowledge itself succeeded — swallowing this would leave the button
138 // live against a stale record with no feedback at all.
139 this.dispatchEvent(
140 new ShowToastEvent({
141 title: "Alert acknowledged",
142 message: "The view could not refresh — reload the page to see the update.",
143 variant: "warning",
144 })
145 );
146 }
147 } catch (e) {
148 this.dispatchEvent(
149 new ShowToastEvent({
150 title: "Error",
151 message: "Failed to acknowledge alert. Please try again.",
152 variant: "error",
153 })
154 );
155 } finally {
156 const next = new Set(this._acknowledging);
157 next.delete(alertId);
158 this._acknowledging = next;
159 }
160 }
161}
OHFY-WMS-UI/force-app/main/default/lwc/transferAlertBanner/transferAlertBanner.js-meta.xml new +19 -0

1import { LightningElement, api, track } from "lwc";
2import { loadStyle } from "lightning/platformResourceLoader";
3import { ShowToastEvent } from "lightning/platformShowToastEvent";
4import formFactor from "@salesforce/client/formFactor";
5import tailwindCSS from "@salesforce/resourceUrl/tailwindCSS_wms";
6import getWarehouseInboundAlerts from "@salesforce/apex/WMS_UI_Wrappers.getWarehouseInboundAlerts";
7import acknowledgeTransferAlert from "@salesforce/apex/WMS_UI_Wrappers.acknowledgeTransferAlert";
8
9const OVERDUE_HOURS = 4;
10const PILL_BASE =
11 "display: inline-block; font-size: 0.6rem; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.04rem; padding: 0.1rem 0.45rem; border-radius: 0.75rem;";
12
13const TYPE_PILL_STYLES = {
14 "Hot Transfer":
15 PILL_BASE +
16 " background: rgb(var(--ohfy-danger, 163 45 45)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface, 255 255 255));",
17 "Close to Code":
18 PILL_BASE +
19 " background: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface-muted, 244 242 240)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-text-muted, 84 84 84));",
20};
21const BAND_PILL_STYLES = {
22 Critical:
23 PILL_BASE +
24 " background: rgb(var(--ohfy-danger, 163 45 45)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface, 255 255 255));",
25 Act:
26 PILL_BASE +
27 " background: rgb(var(--ohfy-accent, 228 242 35)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-primary, 0 0 0));",
28 Monitor:
29 PILL_BASE +
30 " background: rgb(var(--ohfy-surface-muted, 244 242 240)); color: rgb(var(--ohfy-text-muted, 84 84 84));",
31};
32
33export default class TransferAlertQueue extends LightningElement {
34 @api locationId;
35
36 @track _alerts = [];
37 @track isLoading = true;
38 @track _acknowledging = new Set();
39
40 skeletonRows = [1, 2, 3, 4];
41
42 get isMobile() {
43 return formFactor === "Small";
44 }
45
46 connectedCallback() {
47 Promise.all([loadStyle(this, tailwindCSS)]);
48 this._load();
49 }
50
51 async _load() {
52 this.isLoading = true;
53 try {
54 const result = await getWarehouseInboundAlerts({ locationId: this.locationId });
55 this._alerts = result || [];
56 } catch (e) {
57 this._alerts = [];
58 this.dispatchEvent(
59 new ShowToastEvent({
60 title: "Error",
61 message: "Failed to load transfer alerts. Please refresh.",
62 variant: "error",
63 })
64 );
65 } finally {
66 this.isLoading = false;
67 }
68 }
69
70 get isEmpty() {
71 return !this.isLoading && this._alerts.length === 0;
72 }
73
74 get enrichedAlerts() {
75 const now = Date.now();
76 const overdueMs = OVERDUE_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000;
77 const sorted = [...this._alerts].sort((a, b) => {
78 const typeOrder = x => (x.alertType === "Hot Transfer" ? 0 : 1);
79 const bandOrder = x => ({ Critical: 0, Act: 1, Monitor: 2 })[x.urgencyBand] ?? 3;
80 const td = typeOrder(a) - typeOrder(b);
81 if (td !== 0) return td;
82 const bd = bandOrder(a) - bandOrder(b);
83 if (bd !== 0) return bd;
84 const da = a.detectedAt ? new Date(a.detectedAt).getTime() : 0;
85 const db = b.detectedAt ? new Date(b.detectedAt).getTime() : 0;
86 return da - db;
87 });
88
89 return sorted.map(a => {
90 const isHot = a.alertType === "Hot Transfer";
91 const isAcknowledged = a.status === "Acknowledged";
92 const isOverdue =
93 !isAcknowledged &&
94 a.detectedAt &&
95 now - new Date(a.detectedAt).getTime() > overdueMs;
96
97 let daysToCodeLabel = null;
98 if (!isHot && a.daysToCode != null) {
99 const d = a.daysToCode;
100 daysToCodeLabel = d < 0 ? `${Math.abs(d)} day(s) past code` : `${d} day(s) to code`;
101 }
102
103 const rowStyle = isAcknowledged
104 ? "border-bottom: 1px solid rgb(var(--ohfy-border, 177 199 235)); opacity: 0.6;"
105 : "border-bottom: 1px solid rgb(var(--ohfy-border, 177 199 235));";
106
107 return {
108 ...a,
109 isHot,
110 isAcknowledged,
111 isOverdue,
112 showBand: !isHot && !!a.urgencyBand,
113 alertTypeShort: isHot ? "Hot" : "CTC",
114 typePillStyle: TYPE_PILL_STYLES[a.alertType] || TYPE_PILL_STYLES["Close to Code"],
115 bandPillStyle: BAND_PILL_STYLES[a.urgencyBand] || BAND_PILL_STYLES.Monitor,
116 daysToCodeLabel,
117 showDaysToCode: !!daysToCodeLabel,
118 itemName: a.itemName || "—",
119 groupName: a.groupName || "—",
120 originName: a.originName || "—",
121 rowStyle,
122 isAcknowledging: this._acknowledging.has(a.recordId),
123 };
124 });
125 }
126
127 async handleAcknowledge(event) {
128 const alertId = event.currentTarget.dataset.id;
129 if (!alertId || this._acknowledging.has(alertId)) return;
130
131 this._acknowledging = new Set([...this._acknowledging, alertId]);
132 try {
133 await acknowledgeTransferAlert({ alertId });
134 await this._load();
135 } catch (e) {
136 this.dispatchEvent(
137 new ShowToastEvent({
138 title: "Error",
139 message: "Failed to acknowledge alert. Please try again.",
140 variant: "error",
141 })
142 );
143 } finally {
144 const next = new Set(this._acknowledging);
145 next.delete(alertId);
146 this._acknowledging = next;
147 }
148 }
149}

1/**
2 * @description Factory/mapping logic for TransferAlertDTO. Per ADR-0007 rule 5,
3 * factory methods live in a sibling class (no `DTO` suffix), not on
4 * the global DTO itself, so the mapping can evolve without freezing
5 * the managed-package API surface.
6 *
7 * Callers passing alerts from getAlertsForTransferGroup must set
8 * dto.groupName themselves after calling fromSObject — that query
9 * omits Transfer_Group__r.Name to avoid an extra join; accessing an
10 * unqueried relationship field throws SObjectException.
11 */
12@namespaceAccessible
13public with sharing class TransferAlert {
14 @namespaceAccessible
15 public static TransferAlertDTO fromSObject(Transfer_Alert__c a) {
16 TransferAlertDTO dto = new TransferAlertDTO();
17 dto.recordId = a.Id;
18 dto.alertType = a.Alert_Type__c;
19 dto.status = a.Status__c;
20 dto.urgencyBand = a.Urgency_Band__c;
21 dto.daysToCode = a.Days_To_Code__c;
22 dto.caseQuantity = a.Case_Quantity__c;
23 dto.priorityReason = a.Priority_Reason__c;
24 dto.detectedAt = a.Detected_At__c;
25 dto.acknowledgedAt = a.Acknowledged_At__c;
26 dto.acknowledgedByName = a.Acknowledged_By__r != null ? a.Acknowledged_By__r.Name : null;
27 dto.itemName = a.Item__r != null ? a.Item__r.Name : null;
28 dto.itemId = a.Item__c;
29 dto.originName = a.Origin_Location__r != null ? a.Origin_Location__r.Name : null;
30 return dto;
31 }
32}

28 @isTest
29 static void fromSObjectMapsScalarFields() {
30 Location__c origin = new Location__c(
31 Name = 'OriginDTOTest',
32 Location_Code__c = 'ORIG',
33 Is_Active__c = true,
34 Type__c = 'Warehouse'
35 );
36 insert origin;
37 Location__c dest = new Location__c(
38 Name = 'DestDTOTest',
39 Location_Code__c = 'DEST',
40 Is_Active__c = true,
41 Type__c = 'Warehouse'
42 );
43 insert dest;
44 Transfer_Group__c grp = new Transfer_Group__c(
45 Origin_Location__c = origin.Id,
46 New_Location__c = dest.Id,
47 Status__c = 'New',
48 Is_Picked__c = true,
49 Is_External__c = false,
50 Transfer_Date__c = System.today()
51 );
52 insert grp;
53 Transfer_Alert__c alert = new Transfer_Alert__c(
54 Transfer_Group__c = grp.Id,
55 Receiving_Location__c = dest.Id,
56 Origin_Location__c = origin.Id,
57 Alert_Type__c = 'Close to Code',
58 Urgency_Band__c = 'Critical',
59 Status__c = 'New',
60 Days_To_Code__c = 7,
61 Case_Quantity__c = 12,
62 Priority_Reason__c = 'Test reason',
63 Detected_At__c = Datetime.now()
64 );
65 insert alert;
66
67 // Re-query with fields fromSObject accesses (mirrors getAlertsForTransferGroup)
68 Transfer_Alert__c reloaded = [
69 SELECT
70 Id,
71 Alert_Type__c,
72 Status__c,
73 Urgency_Band__c,
74 Days_To_Code__c,
75 Case_Quantity__c,
76 Priority_Reason__c,
77 Detected_At__c,
78 Acknowledged_At__c,
79 Acknowledged_By__c,
80 Item__c,
81 Acknowledged_By__r.Name,
82 Item__r.Name,
83 Origin_Location__r.Name
84 FROM Transfer_Alert__c
85 WHERE Id = :alert.Id
86 ];
87
88 Test.startTest();
89 TransferAlertDTO dto = TransferAlert.fromSObject(reloaded);
90 Test.stopTest();
91
92 Assert.areEqual(alert.Id, dto.recordId, 'recordId maps to Id');
93 Assert.areEqual('Close to Code', dto.alertType, 'alertType');
94 Assert.areEqual('New', dto.status, 'status');
95 Assert.areEqual('Critical', dto.urgencyBand, 'urgencyBand');
96 Assert.areEqual(7, dto.daysToCode, 'daysToCode');
97 Assert.areEqual(12, dto.caseQuantity, 'caseQuantity');
98 Assert.areEqual('Test reason', dto.priorityReason, 'priorityReason');
99 Assert.isNotNull(dto.detectedAt, 'detectedAt populated');
100 Assert.isNull(dto.acknowledgedAt, 'acknowledgedAt null for new alert');
101 Assert.isNull(dto.acknowledgedByName, 'acknowledgedByName null for new alert');
102 Assert.isNull(dto.itemName, 'itemName null when no Item');
103 Assert.isNull(dto.itemId, 'itemId null when no Item');
104 Assert.isNull(dto.groupName, 'groupName null — caller sets it when relationship queried');
105 Assert.areEqual('OriginDTOTest', dto.originName, 'originName from Origin_Location__r.Name');

1/**
2 * @description Executable for Transfer_Alert__c read and write operations (BMS-3768).
3 *
4 * Exposes three @namespaceAccessible operations consumed by WMS_UI_Wrappers:
5 * - getAlertsForTransferGroup — all alerts on a Transfer Group record page banner
6 * - getInboundAlertsForWarehouse — the receiving queue shown on the WMS home page
7 * - acknowledgeAlert — stamps Status__c = Acknowledged with user + timestamp
8 */
9@namespaceAccessible
10public with sharing class E_TransferAlerts {
11 // Severity rank for Urgency_Band__c values; lower = higher priority.
12 // SOQL sorts this field alphabetically (Act < Critical < Monitor), so we sort in Apex instead.
13 private static final Map<String, Integer> URGENCY_RANK = new Map<String, Integer>{
14 'Critical' => 0,
15 'Act' => 1,
16 'Monitor' => 2
17 };
18
19 @namespaceAccessible
20 public static List<Transfer_Alert__c> getAlertsForTransferGroup(Id transferGroupId) {
21 if (transferGroupId == null) {
22 return new List<Transfer_Alert__c>();
23 }
24 List<Transfer_Alert__c> results = (List<Transfer_Alert__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
25 new List<String>{
26 'Id',
27 'Alert_Type__c',
28 'Priority_Reason__c',
29 'Status__c',
30 'Urgency_Band__c',
31 'Days_To_Code__c',
32 'Code_Date__c',
33 'Case_Quantity__c',
34 'Detected_At__c',
35 'Acknowledged_At__c',
36 'Acknowledged_By__c',
37 'Acknowledged_By__r.Name',
38 'Item__r.Name',
39 'Lot__r.Name',
40 'Origin_Location__r.Name',
41 'Transfer__c'
42 },
43 'Transfer_Alert__c'
44 )
45 .filters(new List<String>{ 'Transfer_Group__c = :transferGroupId' })
46 .orderBy(new List<String>{ 'Alert_Type__c DESC', 'Detected_At__c ASC' })
47 .build()
48 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'transferGroupId' => transferGroupId });
49 return sortBySeverity(results);
50 }

52 @namespaceAccessible
53 public static List<Transfer_Alert__c> getInboundAlertsForWarehouse(Id locationId) {
54 if (locationId == null) {
55 return new List<Transfer_Alert__c>();
56 }
57 Datetime cutoff = Datetime.now().addHours(-48);
58 List<Transfer_Alert__c> results = (List<Transfer_Alert__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
59 new List<String>{
60 'Id',
61 'Alert_Type__c',
62 'Priority_Reason__c',
63 'Status__c',
64 'Urgency_Band__c',
65 'Days_To_Code__c',
66 'Code_Date__c',
67 'Case_Quantity__c',
68 'Detected_At__c',
69 'Acknowledged_At__c',
70 'Acknowledged_By__c',
71 'Acknowledged_By__r.Name',
72 'Item__r.Name',
73 'Lot__r.Name',
74 'Origin_Location__r.Name',
75 'Transfer_Group__c',
76 'Transfer_Group__r.Name',
77 'Transfer__c'
78 },
79 'Transfer_Alert__c'
80 )

82 new List<String>{
83 'Receiving_Location__c = :locationId',
84 '(Status__c = \'New\' OR Acknowledged_At__c >= :cutoff)'
85 }
86 )
87 .orderBy(new List<String>{ 'Alert_Type__c DESC', 'Detected_At__c ASC' })
88 .build()
89 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'locationId' => locationId, 'cutoff' => cutoff });
90 return sortBySeverity(results);

93 @namespaceAccessible
94 public static Integer getTransferLineCount(Id transferGroupId) {
95 if (transferGroupId == null) {
96 return 0;
97 }
98 List<AggregateResult> rows = (List<AggregateResult>) QueryService.newBuilder(
99 new List<String>{ 'COUNT(Id)' },
100 'Transfer__c'
101 )
102 .filters(new List<String>{ 'Transfer_Group__c = :transferGroupId' })
103 .build()
104 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'transferGroupId' => transferGroupId });
105 return rows.isEmpty() ? 0 : (Integer) rows[0].get('expr0');
106 }
107
108 // Sort by Alert_Type (Hot Transfer first via DESC is already applied in SOQL), then by
109 // Urgency_Band severity (Critical > Act > Monitor). Runs in Apex because SOQL sorts
110 // Urgency_Band__c alphabetically (Act < Critical < Monitor), which is the wrong order.
111 private static List<Transfer_Alert__c> sortBySeverity(List<Transfer_Alert__c> alerts) {
112 for (Integer i = 0; i < alerts.size() - 1; i++) {
113 for (Integer j = 0; j < alerts.size() - i - 1; j++) {
114 Transfer_Alert__c a = alerts[j];
115 Transfer_Alert__c b = alerts[j + 1];
116 Integer rankA = URGENCY_RANK.containsKey(a.Urgency_Band__c)
117 ? URGENCY_RANK.get(a.Urgency_Band__c)
118 : 99;
119 Integer rankB = URGENCY_RANK.containsKey(b.Urgency_Band__c)
120 ? URGENCY_RANK.get(b.Urgency_Band__c)
121 : 99;
122 if (rankA > rankB) {
123 alerts[j] = b;
124 alerts[j + 1] = a;
125 }
126 }
127 }
128 return alerts;
129 }
130
131 @namespaceAccessible
132 public static void acknowledgeAlert(Id alertId) {
133 if (alertId == null) {
134 return;
135 }
136 try {
137 // First acknowledger wins: a re-acknowledge (concurrent user, or a stale UI that
138 // still shows the button) must not overwrite the original audit stamp.
139 List<Transfer_Alert__c> current = (List<Transfer_Alert__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
140 new List<String>{ 'Id', 'Status__c' },
141 'Transfer_Alert__c'
142 )
143 .filters(new List<String>{ 'Id = :alertId' })
144 .build()
145 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'alertId' => alertId });
146 if (current.isEmpty() || current[0].Status__c == 'Acknowledged') {
147 return;
148 }
149 DmlService.doUpdate(
150 new List<Transfer_Alert__c>{
151 new Transfer_Alert__c(
152 Id = alertId,
153 Status__c = 'Acknowledged',
154 Acknowledged_By__c = UserInfo.getUserId(),
155 Acknowledged_At__c = Datetime.now()
156 )
157 }
158 );
159 } catch (Exception e) {
160 Logger.getInstance()
161 .error('E_TransferAlerts.acknowledgeAlert failed for ' + alertId, e);
162 Logger.getInstance().flush();
163 throw e;
164 }
165 }

1/**
2 * @description Sends Salesforce custom notifications for Transfer_Alert__c records (BMS-3768).
3 *
4 * Alerts are grouped by Transfer_Group__c before sending — one notification per Transfer Group,
5 * summarising all flagged lines. This avoids notification spam when a single dispatch produces
6 * multiple close-to-code alerts. Hot Transfer alerts are one-per-group by construction so they
7 * collapse naturally.
8 *
9 * Recipient resolution mirrors E_Staging.usersWithSupervisorPermission: locate the
10 * WMS_Transfer_Alert_Receiver custom permission, walk SetupEntityAccess to find the permission
11 * sets that carry it, then collect all users assigned to those permission sets. All three
12 * setup-object queries run in SYSTEM_MODE (rule 6 — cross-namespace setup reads).
13 *
14 * Advisory only: any failure is logged and swallowed so a notification outage never blocks
15 * the dispatch or the hot-transfer flag save.
16 */
17@namespaceAccessible
18public with sharing class S_TransferAlertNotifier {
19 @TestVisible
20 private static String NOTIF_TYPE_DEV_NAME = 'WMS_Transfer_Alert';
21 @TestVisible
22 private static final String CUSTOM_PERMISSION_NAME = 'WMS_Transfer_Alert_Receiver';
23
24 @TestVisible
25 private static Set<String> recipientOverride;
26
27 // Transaction-level caches: the recipient set and notification type are invariant within
28 // a transaction, and notifyReceivingUsers can run twice per Transfer_Group update (CTC
29 // dispatch + hot toggle in one save) — without these that costs 8 setup-object SOQL.
30 @TestVisible
31 private static Set<String> cachedRecipients;
32 @TestVisible
33 private static Id cachedNotifTypeId;
34 @TestVisible
35 private static Boolean notifTypeResolved = false;
36
37 // Tracks input batch size (0 for empty/null, -1 if never called). Test-only.
38 @TestVisible
39 static Integer lastNotifyBatchSize = -1;
40
41 // Tracks how many per-group notifications were attempted in the last call. Test-only.
42 @TestVisible
43 static Integer lastGroupCount = -1;
44
45 /**
46 * @description Sends one WMS Transfer Alert custom notification per Transfer Group for the
47 * supplied alerts. No-op when the notification type is not deployed or no users
48 * hold the WMS_Transfer_Alert_Receiver custom permission.
49 * @param alerts Transfer_Alert__c records with Transfer_Group__c, Transfer_Group__r.Name,
50 * Origin_Location__r.Name, Urgency_Band__c, Alert_Type__c, Priority_Reason__c,
51 * Days_To_Code__c, and Case_Quantity__c populated (caller must query these).
52 */
53 @namespaceAccessible
54 public static void notifyReceivingUsers(List<Transfer_Alert__c> alerts) {
55 lastNotifyBatchSize = (alerts == null ? 0 : alerts.size());
56 if (alerts == null || alerts.isEmpty()) {
57 return;
58 }
59
60 try {
61 Set<String> recipients = (recipientOverride != null)
62 ? recipientOverride
63 : resolveRecipients();
64 if (recipients.isEmpty()) {
65 return;
66 }
67 Id notifTypeId = getNotificationTypeId();
68 if (notifTypeId == null) {
69 return;
70 }
71
72 // Group by Transfer Group (null key = alerts with no parent group).
73 Map<Id, List<Transfer_Alert__c>> byGroup = new Map<Id, List<Transfer_Alert__c>>();
74 for (Transfer_Alert__c alert : alerts) {
75 Id groupId = alert.Transfer_Group__c;
76 if (!byGroup.containsKey(groupId)) {
77 byGroup.put(groupId, new List<Transfer_Alert__c>());
78 }
79 byGroup.get(groupId).add(alert);
80 }
81
82 lastGroupCount = byGroup.size();
83 for (Id groupId : byGroup.keySet()) {
84 sendForGroup(byGroup.get(groupId), groupId, notifTypeId, recipients);
85 }
86 } catch (Exception e) {
87 Logger.getInstance().error('S_TransferAlertNotifier.notifyReceivingUsers failed', e);
88 Logger.getInstance().flush();
89 }
90 }
91
92 private static void sendForGroup(
93 List<Transfer_Alert__c> groupAlerts,
94 Id groupId,
95 Id notifTypeId,
96 Set<String> recipients
97 ) {
98 try {
99 String title = buildGroupTitle(groupAlerts);
100 String body = buildGroupBody(groupAlerts);
101 // Send to the Transfer Group record so the bell click lands on the right page.
102 Id targetId = (groupId != null) ? groupId : groupAlerts[0].Id;
103
104 Messaging.CustomNotification notification = new Messaging.CustomNotification();
105 notification.setTitle(title);
106 notification.setBody(body);
107 notification.setNotificationTypeId(notifTypeId);
108 notification.setTargetId(targetId);
109 notification.send(recipients);
110 } catch (Exception e) {
111 Logger.getInstance()
112 .error('S_TransferAlertNotifier.sendForGroup failed for group ' + groupId, e);
113 Logger.getInstance().flush();
114 }
115 }
116
117 private static String buildGroupTitle(List<Transfer_Alert__c> groupAlerts) {
118 String tgName = null;
119 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : groupAlerts) {
120 if (a.Transfer_Group__r != null && a.Transfer_Group__r.Name != null) {
121 tgName = a.Transfer_Group__r.Name;
122 break;
123 }
124 }
125
126 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : groupAlerts) {
127 if (a.Alert_Type__c == 'Hot Transfer') {
128 String reason = a.Priority_Reason__c != null
129 ? a.Priority_Reason__c
130 : 'Priority shipment';
131 return 'Hot Transfer' + (tgName != null ? ': ' + tgName : '') + ' — ' + reason;
132 }
133 }
134
135 Integer count = groupAlerts.size();
136 return 'Transfer Alert' +
137 (tgName != null ? ': ' + tgName : '') +
138 ' — ' +
139 count +
140 ' item' +
141 (count == 1 ? '' : 's') +
142 ' close to code';
143 }
144
145 private static String buildGroupBody(List<Transfer_Alert__c> groupAlerts) {
146 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : groupAlerts) {
147 if (a.Alert_Type__c == 'Hot Transfer') {
148 String originName = (a.Origin_Location__r != null &&
149 a.Origin_Location__r.Name != null)
150 ? a.Origin_Location__r.Name
151 : 'Origin warehouse';
152 return 'From ' + originName + '. Expedited handling required.';
153 }
154 }
155
156 // Find most urgent CTC alert (fewest days to code).
157 Transfer_Alert__c mostUrgent = null;
158 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : groupAlerts) {
159 if (
160 mostUrgent == null ||
161 (a.Days_To_Code__c != null &&
162 (mostUrgent.Days_To_Code__c == null ||
163 a.Days_To_Code__c < mostUrgent.Days_To_Code__c))
164 ) {
165 mostUrgent = a;
166 }
167 }
168
169 if (mostUrgent == null) {
170 return 'Review inbound transfer for close-to-code items.';
171 }
172
173 Integer days = mostUrgent.Days_To_Code__c != null
174 ? mostUrgent.Days_To_Code__c.intValue()
175 : 0;
176 String band = mostUrgent.Urgency_Band__c != null ? mostUrgent.Urgency_Band__c : 'Monitor';
177 String originName = (mostUrgent.Origin_Location__r != null &&
178 mostUrgent.Origin_Location__r.Name != null)
179 ? mostUrgent.Origin_Location__r.Name
180 : 'Origin warehouse';
181
182 String body = 'Most urgent: ' + days + ' day(s) (' + band + ') from ' + originName + '.';
183 if (groupAlerts.size() > 1) {
184 body += ' ' + groupAlerts.size() + ' items flagged.';
185 }
186 return body;
187 }
188
189 private static Id getNotificationTypeId() {
190 if (notifTypeResolved) {
191 return cachedNotifTypeId;
192 }
193 // SYSTEM_MODE: setup-object lookup for notification delivery (rule 6 in docs/security/mode-selection.md)
194 List<CustomNotificationType> types = (List<CustomNotificationType>) QueryService.newBuilder(
195 new List<String>{ 'Id' },
196 'CustomNotificationType'
197 )
198 .filters(new List<String>{ 'DeveloperName = :devName' })
199 .build()
200 .execute(
201 new Map<String, Object>{ 'devName' => NOTIF_TYPE_DEV_NAME },
202 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
203 );
204 cachedNotifTypeId = types.isEmpty() ? null : types[0].Id;
205 notifTypeResolved = true;
206 return cachedNotifTypeId;
207 }
208
209 private static Set<String> resolveRecipients() {
210 if (cachedRecipients != null) {
211 return cachedRecipients;
212 }
213 Set<String> userIds = new Set<String>();
214
215 // SYSTEM_MODE: setup-object lookups for notification recipient resolution (rule 6 in docs/security/mode-selection.md)
216 List<CustomPermission> perms = (List<CustomPermission>) QueryService.newBuilder(
217 new List<String>{ 'Id' },
218 'CustomPermission'
219 )
220 .filters(new List<String>{ 'DeveloperName = :devName' })
221 .build()
222 .execute(
223 new Map<String, Object>{ 'devName' => CUSTOM_PERMISSION_NAME },
224 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
225 );
226 if (perms.isEmpty()) {
227 cachedRecipients = userIds;
228 return userIds;
229 }
230 Set<Id> permIds = new Map<Id, CustomPermission>(perms).keySet();
231
232 // SYSTEM_MODE: setup-object lookups for notification recipient resolution (rule 6 in docs/security/mode-selection.md)
233 List<SetupEntityAccess> access = (List<SetupEntityAccess>) QueryService.newBuilder(
234 new List<String>{ 'Id', 'ParentId' },
235 'SetupEntityAccess'
236 )
237 .filters(
238 new List<String>{ 'SetupEntityType = :entityType', 'SetupEntityId IN :permIds' }
239 )
240 .build()
241 .execute(
242 new Map<String, Object>{ 'entityType' => 'CustomPermission', 'permIds' => permIds },
243 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
244 );
245 if (access.isEmpty()) {
246 cachedRecipients = userIds;
247 return userIds;
248 }
249 Set<Id> permissionSetIds = new Set<Id>();
250 for (SetupEntityAccess sea : access) {
251 permissionSetIds.add(sea.ParentId);
252 }
253
254 // SYSTEM_MODE: setup-object lookups for notification recipient resolution (rule 6 in docs/security/mode-selection.md)
255 List<PermissionSetAssignment> assignments = (List<PermissionSetAssignment>) QueryService.newBuilder(
256 new List<String>{ 'Id', 'AssigneeId' },
257 'PermissionSetAssignment'
258 )
259 .filters(new List<String>{ 'PermissionSetId IN :permissionSetIds' })
260 .build()
261 .execute(
262 new Map<String, Object>{ 'permissionSetIds' => permissionSetIds },
263 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
264 );
265 for (PermissionSetAssignment psa : assignments) {
266 userIds.add(psa.AssigneeId);
267 }
268 cachedRecipients = userIds;
269 return userIds;
270 }

1/**
2 * @description Internal Transfer Alerts evaluator (BMS-3768).
3 *
4 * When a Transfer Group is dispatched (New -> In Progress), this service inspects each of its
5 * Transfer__c lines and decides whether the receiving warehouse should be alerted that the inbound
6 * product is close to code. It builds one Transfer_Alert__c per flagged line; the caller
7 * (TransferGroupTriggerService.publishTransferAlerts) upserts them synchronously so no platform
8 * event is needed.
9 *
10 * Close-to-code classification reuses the existing close-to-code config — CTC_Threshold__mdt
11 * (Warehouse_Code__c, Item_Category__c, Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c, Act_Band_Days__c,
12 * Critical_Band_Days__c) — resolved against the RECEIVING warehouse's location code so urgency
13 * reflects the destination's code-date tolerance (Gulf FL vs AL via per-warehouse rows). No new
14 * threshold is introduced. The shipping lot is the soonest-expiring sellable lot of the line's item
15 * at the origin location (FEFO): the lot most at risk of crossing code before it can be sold through.
16 *
17 * Bulk-safe: every supporting query is set-based and the evaluation is per-line. Non-lot-tracked
18 * items (no sellable lot with an expiration at origin) produce no alert.
19 */
20public with sharing class S_TransferAlerts {
21 @TestVisible
22 private static List<CTC_Threshold__mdt> thresholdOverride;
23 @TestVisible
24 private static Datetime detectedAtOverride;
25 @TestVisible
26 private static Boolean failForTest = false;
27
28 private class TransferAlertException extends Exception {
29 }
30
31 @TestVisible
32 private static final String BAND_CRITICAL = 'Critical';
33 @TestVisible
34 private static final String BAND_ACT = 'Act';
35 @TestVisible
36 private static final String BAND_MONITOR = 'Monitor';
37
38 /**
39 * @description Builds Transfer_Alert__c records for every close-to-code line on the supplied
40 * dispatched Transfer Groups. Returns an empty list (never null) when nothing
41 * qualifies. Records are keyed on External_Id__c ('transfer-alert:{lineId}:{lotId}')
42 * for idempotent upsert by the caller.
43 * @param transferGroupIds Transfer_Group__c ids that just advanced New -> In Progress
44 * @return one alert per flagged Transfer line (item within the receiving warehouse's CTC band)
45 */
46 public static List<Transfer_Alert__c> buildAlerts(Set<Id> transferGroupIds) {
47 List<Transfer_Alert__c> alerts = new List<Transfer_Alert__c>();
48 if (Test.isRunningTest() && failForTest) {
49 throw new TransferAlertException('Forced failure for publish catch-path coverage');
50 }
51 if (transferGroupIds == null || transferGroupIds.isEmpty()) {
52 return alerts;
53 }
54
55 Map<Id, Transfer_Group__c> groupsById = loadGroups(transferGroupIds);
56 if (groupsById.isEmpty()) {
57 return alerts;
58 }
59 List<Transfer__c> lines = loadLines(transferGroupIds);
60 if (lines.isEmpty()) {
61 return alerts;
62 }
63
64 Set<Id> itemIds = new Set<Id>();
65 Set<Id> originIds = new Set<Id>();
66 Set<Id> destIds = new Set<Id>();
67 for (Transfer_Group__c grp : groupsById.values()) {
68 if (grp.Origin_Location__c != null) {
69 originIds.add(grp.Origin_Location__c);
70 }
71 if (grp.New_Location__c != null) {
72 destIds.add(grp.New_Location__c);
73 }
74 }
75 for (Transfer__c line : lines) {
76 if (line.Item__c != null) {
77 itemIds.add(line.Item__c);
78 }
79 }
80 if (itemIds.isEmpty() || originIds.isEmpty() || destIds.isEmpty()) {
81 return alerts;
82 }
83
84 Map<String, Lot_Inventory__c> earliestLotByOriginItem = loadEarliestLots(
85 itemIds,
86 originIds
87 );
88 if (earliestLotByOriginItem.isEmpty()) {
89 return alerts; // nothing lot-tracked / nothing with an expiry at origin
90 }
91 Map<Id, String> codeByLocation = loadLocationCodes(destIds);
92 Map<Id, String> categoryByItem = loadItemCategories(itemIds);
93 List<CTC_Threshold__mdt> thresholds = getThresholds();
94
95 Datetime detectedAt = (detectedAtOverride != null) ? detectedAtOverride : Datetime.now();
96 Date evalDate = detectedAt.date();
97
98 for (Transfer__c line : lines) {
99 Transfer_Group__c grp = groupsById.get(line.Transfer_Group__c);
100 if (
101 grp == null ||
102 grp.Origin_Location__c == null ||
103 grp.New_Location__c == null ||
104 line.Item__c == null
105 ) {
106 continue;
107 }
108 Lot_Inventory__c fefo = earliestLotByOriginItem.get(
109 grp.Origin_Location__c + '|' + line.Item__c
110 );
111 if (fefo == null || fefo.Lot__r == null || fefo.Lot__r.Expiration_Date__c == null) {
112 continue; // non-lot-tracked, or no sellable lot with a code date at origin
113 }
114
115 Date codeDate = fefo.Lot__r.Expiration_Date__c;
116 Integer daysToCode = evalDate.daysBetween(codeDate);
117 CTC_Threshold__mdt threshold = resolveThreshold(
118 thresholds,
119 codeByLocation.get(grp.New_Location__c),
120 categoryByItem.get(line.Item__c)
121 );
122 if (
123 threshold == null ||
124 threshold.Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c == null ||
125 daysToCode > threshold.Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c
126 ) {
127 continue; // not close to code under the receiving warehouse's band
128 }
129
130 alerts.add(
131 new Transfer_Alert__c(
132 External_Id__c = 'transfer-alert:' + line.Id + ':' + fefo.Lot__c,
133 Transfer__c = line.Id,
134 Transfer_Group__c = grp.Id,
135 Item__c = line.Item__c,
136 Lot__c = fefo.Lot__c,
137 Receiving_Location__c = grp.New_Location__c,
138 Origin_Location__c = grp.Origin_Location__c,
139 Case_Quantity__c = line.Transfer_Quantity_Cases__c,
140 Code_Date__c = codeDate,
141 Days_To_Code__c = daysToCode,
142 Urgency_Band__c = deriveBand(daysToCode, threshold),
143 Detected_At__c = detectedAt,
144 Alert_Type__c = 'Close to Code',
145 Status__c = 'New'
146 )
147 );
148 }
149 return alerts;
150 }
151
152 /**
153 * @description Urgency band for a close-to-code line, mirroring the CTC engine's priority bands:
154 * Critical within Critical_Band_Days__c, Act within Act_Band_Days__c, else Monitor.
155 */
156 @TestVisible
157 private static String deriveBand(Integer daysToCode, CTC_Threshold__mdt threshold) {
158 if (
159 threshold.Critical_Band_Days__c != null &&
160 daysToCode <= threshold.Critical_Band_Days__c
161 ) {
162 return BAND_CRITICAL;
163 }
164 if (threshold.Act_Band_Days__c != null && daysToCode <= threshold.Act_Band_Days__c) {
165 return BAND_ACT;
166 }
167 return BAND_MONITOR;
168 }
169
170 /**
171 * @description Resolves the most specific active threshold for a receiving warehouse code + item
172 * category, falling back through wildcards: exact, category-wildcard,
173 * warehouse-wildcard, then the all-wildcard default. Same precedence as
174 * S_CTCSuggestionEngine.resolveThreshold so transfer urgency and on-hand
175 * suggestions classify code dates identically.
176 */
177 @TestVisible
178 private static CTC_Threshold__mdt resolveThreshold(
179 List<CTC_Threshold__mdt> thresholds,
180 String warehouseCode,
181 String category
182 ) {
183 CTC_Threshold__mdt exact = null;
184 CTC_Threshold__mdt whWild = null;
185 CTC_Threshold__mdt catWild = null;
186 CTC_Threshold__mdt allWild = null;
187 for (CTC_Threshold__mdt t : thresholds) {
188 if (t.Is_Active__c != true) {
189 continue;
190 }
191 Boolean whMatch = t.Warehouse_Code__c == warehouseCode;
192 Boolean whIsWild = t.Warehouse_Code__c == '*';
193 Boolean catMatch = t.Item_Category__c == category;
194 Boolean catIsWild = t.Item_Category__c == '*';
195 if (whMatch && catMatch) {
196 exact = t;
197 } else if (whMatch && catIsWild) {
198 whWild = t;
199 } else if (whIsWild && catMatch) {
200 catWild = t;
201 } else if (whIsWild && catIsWild) {
202 allWild = t;
203 }
204 }
205 if (exact != null) {
206 return exact;
207 }
208 if (catWild != null) {
209 return catWild;
210 }
211 if (whWild != null) {
212 return whWild;
213 }
214 return allWild;
215 }
216
217 // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
218 // Loaders
219 // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
220
221 private static Map<Id, Transfer_Group__c> loadGroups(Set<Id> transferGroupIds) {
222 return new Map<Id, Transfer_Group__c>(
223 (List<Transfer_Group__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
224 new List<String>{ 'Id', 'Origin_Location__c', 'New_Location__c' },
225 'Transfer_Group__c'
226 )
227 .filters(new List<String>{ 'Id IN :transferGroupIds' })
228 .build()
229 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'transferGroupIds' => transferGroupIds })
230 );
231 }
232
233 private static List<Transfer__c> loadLines(Set<Id> transferGroupIds) {
234 return (List<Transfer__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
235 new List<String>{
236 'Id',
237 'Item__c',
238 'Transfer_Group__c',
239 'Transfer_Quantity_Cases__c'
240 },
241 'Transfer__c'
242 )
243 .filters(new List<String>{ 'Transfer_Group__c IN :transferGroupIds' })
244 .build()
245 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'transferGroupIds' => transferGroupIds });
246 }
247
248 /**
249 * @description The soonest-expiring sellable, on-hand lot of each item at each origin location
250 * (FEFO). Keyed 'originLocationId|itemId'. Computes the minimum in Apex so the
251 * method does not depend on SOQL ordering of the lot-inventory query.
252 */
253 private static Map<String, Lot_Inventory__c> loadEarliestLots(
254 Set<Id> itemIds,
255 Set<Id> originIds
256 ) {
257 Map<String, Lot_Inventory__c> earliest = new Map<String, Lot_Inventory__c>();
258 List<Lot_Inventory__c> lotInvs = (List<Lot_Inventory__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
259 new List<String>{
260 'Id',
261 'Lot__c',
262 'Lot__r.Expiration_Date__c',
263 'Inventory__r.Item__c',
264 'Inventory__r.Location__c',
265 'Quantity_On_Hand__c'
266 },
267 'Lot_Inventory__c'
268 )
269 .filters(
270 new List<String>{
271 'Inventory__r.Item__c IN :itemIds',
272 'Inventory__r.Location__c IN :originIds',
273 'Quantity_On_Hand__c > 0',
274 'Lot__r.Is_Sellable__c = true',
275 'Lot__r.Expiration_Date__c != null'
276 }
277 )
278 .build()
279 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'itemIds' => itemIds, 'originIds' => originIds });
280 for (Lot_Inventory__c li : lotInvs) {
281 if (
282 li.Inventory__r == null ||
283 li.Inventory__r.Item__c == null ||
284 li.Inventory__r.Location__c == null ||
285 li.Lot__r == null ||
286 li.Lot__r.Expiration_Date__c == null
287 ) {
288 continue;
289 }
290 String key = li.Inventory__r.Location__c + '|' + li.Inventory__r.Item__c;
291 Lot_Inventory__c incumbent = earliest.get(key);
292 if (
293 incumbent == null ||
294 li.Lot__r.Expiration_Date__c < incumbent.Lot__r.Expiration_Date__c
295 ) {
296 earliest.put(key, li);
297 }
298 }
299 return earliest;
300 }
301
302 private static Map<Id, String> loadLocationCodes(Set<Id> locationIds) {
303 Map<Id, String> result = new Map<Id, String>();
304 List<Location__c> locs = (List<Location__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
305 new List<String>{ 'Id', 'Location_Code__c' },
306 'Location__c'
307 )
308 .filters(new List<String>{ 'Id IN :locationIds' })
309 .build()
310 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'locationIds' => locationIds });
311 for (Location__c loc : locs) {
312 result.put(loc.Id, loc.Location_Code__c);
313 }
314 return result;
315 }
316
317 private static Map<Id, String> loadItemCategories(Set<Id> itemIds) {
318 Map<Id, String> result = new Map<Id, String>();
319 List<Item__c> items = (List<Item__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
320 new List<String>{ 'Id', 'Category__c' },
321 'Item__c'
322 )
323 .filters(new List<String>{ 'Id IN :itemIds' })
324 .build()
325 .execute(new Map<String, Object>{ 'itemIds' => itemIds });
326 for (Item__c item : items) {
327 result.put(item.Id, item.Category__c);
328 }
329 return result;
330 }
331
332 private static List<CTC_Threshold__mdt> getThresholds() {
333 if (thresholdOverride != null) {
334 return thresholdOverride;
335 }
336 // SYSTEM_MODE: Custom Metadata Type lookup — configuration, not user data (rule 1 in
337 // docs/security/mode-selection.md).
338 return (List<CTC_Threshold__mdt>) QueryService.newBuilder(
339 new List<String>{
340 'Warehouse_Code__c',
341 'Item_Category__c',
342 'Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c',
343 'Act_Band_Days__c',
344 'Critical_Band_Days__c',
345 'Is_Active__c'
346 },
347 'CTC_Threshold__mdt'
348 )
349 .build()
350 .execute(new Map<String, Object>(), AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE);
351 }

147 @isTest
148 static void inBandCriticalLotBuildsAlert() {
149 Id origin = warehouse('McCalla', 'MCAL').Id;
150 Id dest = warehouse('Milton', 'MILTN').Id;
151 Id itemId = lotTrackedItem('CTC-CRIT');
152 Id invId = inventory(origin, itemId);
153 lotInventory(invId, lot(itemId, System.today() + 5), 100);
154 Transfer_Group__c grp = newGroup(origin, dest);
155 line(grp.Id, itemId, 12);
156
157 S_TransferAlerts.thresholdOverride = wildcardThreshold();

451 @isTest
452 static void resolveThresholdPrecedenceAndDeriveBand() {
453 CTC_Threshold__mdt exact = new CTC_Threshold__mdt(
454 Warehouse_Code__c = 'MILTN',
455 Item_Category__c = 'Beer',
456 Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c = 30,
457 Act_Band_Days__c = 14,
458 Critical_Band_Days__c = 7,
459 Is_Active__c = true
460 );
461 CTC_Threshold__mdt catWild = new CTC_Threshold__mdt(
462 Warehouse_Code__c = '*',
463 Item_Category__c = 'Beer',
464 Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c = 20,
465 Is_Active__c = true
466 );
467 CTC_Threshold__mdt whWild = new CTC_Threshold__mdt(
468 Warehouse_Code__c = 'MILTN',
469 Item_Category__c = '*',
470 Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c = 25,
471 Is_Active__c = true
472 );
473 CTC_Threshold__mdt allWild = new CTC_Threshold__mdt(
474 Warehouse_Code__c = '*',
475 Item_Category__c = '*',
476 Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c = 10,
477 Is_Active__c = true
478 );
479 CTC_Threshold__mdt inactive = new CTC_Threshold__mdt(
480 Warehouse_Code__c = 'MILTN',
481 Item_Category__c = 'Beer',
482 Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c = 99,
483 Is_Active__c = false
484 );
485 List<CTC_Threshold__mdt> all = new List<CTC_Threshold__mdt>{
486 allWild,
487 whWild,
488 catWild,
489 exact,
490 inactive
491 };
492
493 // Exact match wins and inactive same-key row is ignored.
494 System.Assert.areEqual(
495 30,
496 S_TransferAlerts.resolveThreshold(all, 'MILTN', 'Beer').Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c,
497 'Exact (warehouse+category) match should win'
498 );
499 // No exact -> category-wildcard beats warehouse-wildcard.
500 System.Assert.areEqual(
501 25,
502 S_TransferAlerts.resolveThreshold(
503 new List<CTC_Threshold__mdt>{ allWild, whWild, catWild },
504 'MILTN',
505 'Wine'
506 )
507 .Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c,
508 'No exact: category-wildcard (wh match) precedence'
509 );
510 // Only the all-wildcard applies for an unknown warehouse + category.
511 System.Assert.areEqual(
512 10,
513 S_TransferAlerts.resolveThreshold(all, 'NOWHERE', 'Spirits')
514 .Code_Date_Threshold_Days__c,
515 'Unknown warehouse/category falls back to all-wildcard'
516 );
517 // No active row matches.
518 System.Assert.isNull(
519 S_TransferAlerts.resolveThreshold(
520 new List<CTC_Threshold__mdt>{ inactive },
521 'MILTN',
522 'Beer'
523 ),
524 'Only an inactive row -> no threshold'
525 );
526
527 // deriveBand edges, including null band fields.
528 System.Assert.areEqual(
529 'Critical',
530 S_TransferAlerts.deriveBand(7, exact),
531 '7 <= critical 7'
532 );
533 System.Assert.areEqual('Act', S_TransferAlerts.deriveBand(8, exact), '8 within act 14');
534 System.Assert.areEqual('Monitor', S_TransferAlerts.deriveBand(15, exact), '15 > act 14');
535 System.Assert.areEqual(
536 'Monitor',
537 S_TransferAlerts.deriveBand(1, catWild),
538 'Null band fields fall through to Monitor'
539 );
540 }

93111 public void afterUndelete(List<SObject> newList, Set<String> enabledMethods) {

95113
114 /**
115 * @description Internal Transfer Alerts (BMS-3768). When a Transfer Group is dispatched
116 * (New -> In Progress), upserts a Transfer_Alert__c for each line shipping close-to-code
117 * product so the receiving warehouse is notified to prioritise dock processing. Urgency is
118 * classified against the RECEIVING warehouse's CTC_Threshold__mdt band (S_TransferAlerts).
119 *
120 * Alerts are advisory: any failure to build or upsert them is logged and swallowed so it can
121 * never block the dispatch transition. Only the New -> In Progress edge is gated — status cannot
122 * move backwards, so the alert fires once per group. Records are keyed on External_Id__c for
123 * idempotency.
124 *
125 * @param newTransferGroups Transfer_Group__c records with new values
126 * @param oldTransferGroups Transfer_Group__c records with previous values
127 */
128 @TestVisible
129 private void publishTransferAlerts(
130 List<Transfer_Group__c> newTransferGroups,
131 List<Transfer_Group__c> oldTransferGroups
132 ) {
133 Map<Id, Transfer_Group__c> oldMap = new Map<Id, Transfer_Group__c>(oldTransferGroups);
134 Set<Id> dispatchedGroupIds = new Set<Id>();
135 for (Transfer_Group__c tg : newTransferGroups) {
136 Transfer_Group__c old = oldMap.get(tg.Id);
137 if (tg.Status__c == 'In Progress' && old != null && old.Status__c == 'New') {
138 dispatchedGroupIds.add(tg.Id);
139 }
140 }
141 if (dispatchedGroupIds.isEmpty()) {
142 return;
143 }
144
145 try {
146 List<Transfer_Alert__c> alerts = S_TransferAlerts.buildAlerts(dispatchedGroupIds);
147 if (alerts.isEmpty()) {
148 return;
149 }
150 // SYSTEM_MODE: Trigger context — alert records are system-detected, not user-initiated
151 // (rule 4 in docs/security/mode-selection.md). Upsert on External_Id__c is idempotent.
152 DmlService.doUpsert(
153 alerts,
154 Transfer_Alert__c.External_Id__c,
155 true,
156 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
157 );
158 Set<String> externalIds = new Set<String>();
159 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : alerts) {
160 externalIds.add(a.External_Id__c);
161 }
162 List<Transfer_Alert__c> alertsForNotify = (List<Transfer_Alert__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
163 new List<String>{
164 'Id',
165 'Alert_Type__c',
166 'Urgency_Band__c',
167 'Days_To_Code__c',
168 'Case_Quantity__c',
169 'Priority_Reason__c',
170 'Transfer_Group__c',
171 'Transfer_Group__r.Name',
172 'Item__r.Name',
173 'Origin_Location__r.Name'
174 },
175 'Transfer_Alert__c'
176 )
177 .filters(new List<String>{ 'External_Id__c IN :externalIds' })
178 .build()
179 // SYSTEM_MODE: post-upsert re-query in trigger context — same rule-4 rationale.
180 .execute(
181 new Map<String, Object>{ 'externalIds' => externalIds },
182 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
183 );
184 S_TransferAlertNotifier.notifyReceivingUsers(alertsForNotify);
185 } catch (Exception e) {
186 // Alerts are advisory and must never block the dispatch — log and swallow.
187 Logger.getInstance()
188 .error('TransferGroupTriggerService.publishTransferAlerts failed', e);
189 Logger.getInstance().flush();
190 }
191 }
192
193 /**
194 * @description Hot Transfer Alerts (BMS-3768). When a Transfer Group's Is_Hot_Transfer__c is
195 * toggled from false to true, creates a Transfer_Alert__c of type 'Hot Transfer' directly for
196 * the receiving warehouse and sends a platform notification to receiving staff.
197 *
198 * Idempotent: the alert is upserted on External_Id__c = 'hot-alert:{groupId}', so toggling
199 * the flag off and on again does not create a duplicate. Advisory: failures are logged and
200 * swallowed so they never block the field save.
201 */
202 @TestVisible
203 private void publishHotTransferAlerts(
204 List<Transfer_Group__c> newTransferGroups,
205 List<Transfer_Group__c> oldTransferGroups
206 ) {
207 Map<Id, Transfer_Group__c> oldMap = new Map<Id, Transfer_Group__c>(oldTransferGroups);
208 List<Transfer_Alert__c> hotAlerts = new List<Transfer_Alert__c>();
209 Boolean suppressionLogged = false;
210 for (Transfer_Group__c tg : newTransferGroups) {
211 Transfer_Group__c old = oldMap.get(tg.Id);
212 Boolean wasHot = old != null && old.Is_Hot_Transfer__c == true;
213 Boolean isHot = tg.Is_Hot_Transfer__c == true;
214 if (!wasHot && isHot && tg.New_Location__c != null) {
215 hotAlerts.add(
216 new Transfer_Alert__c(
217 External_Id__c = 'hot-alert:' + tg.Id,
218 Transfer_Group__c = tg.Id,
219 Receiving_Location__c = tg.New_Location__c,
220 Origin_Location__c = tg.Origin_Location__c,
221 Alert_Type__c = 'Hot Transfer',
222 Priority_Reason__c = tg.Hot_Transfer_Priority_Reason__c,
223 Status__c = 'New',
224 Detected_At__c = Datetime.now()
225 )
226 );
227 } else if (!wasHot && isHot) {
228 // New_Location__c is blank at toggle time — alert suppressed. The hot-transfer edge
229 // only fires on the false→true transition so filling in the location later will not
230 // retroactively create this alert.
231 Logger.getInstance()
232 .error(
233 'TransferGroupTriggerService.publishHotTransferAlerts: Transfer Group ' +
234 tg.Id +
235 ' marked hot with no New_Location__c — alert suppressed',
236 null
237 );
238 suppressionLogged = true;
239 }
240 }
241 // Flush before the happy path: in a mixed batch (one suppressed, one valid) the
242 // success path below never flushes, which would silently discard this entry.
243 if (suppressionLogged) {
244 Logger.getInstance().flush();
245 }
246 if (hotAlerts.isEmpty()) {
247 return;
248 }
249
250 try {
251 // SYSTEM_MODE: Trigger context — alert records are system-detected, not user-initiated
252 // (rule 4 in docs/security/mode-selection.md). Upsert on External_Id__c is idempotent.
253 DmlService.doUpsert(
254 hotAlerts,
255 Transfer_Alert__c.External_Id__c,
256 true,
257 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
258 );
259 // Re-query by External_Id__c — Database.upsert does not back-fill Id on the
260 // in-memory record for UPDATE legs (external-Id match), so collecting a.Id here
261 // would miss re-toggles. External_Id__c is always set before the upsert.
262 Set<String> externalIds = new Set<String>();
263 for (Transfer_Alert__c a : hotAlerts) {
264 externalIds.add(a.External_Id__c);
265 }
266 List<Transfer_Alert__c> alertsForNotify = (List<Transfer_Alert__c>) QueryService.newBuilder(
267 new List<String>{
268 'Id',
269 'Alert_Type__c',
270 'Priority_Reason__c',
271 'Case_Quantity__c',
272 'Transfer_Group__c',
273 'Transfer_Group__r.Name',
274 'Origin_Location__r.Name'
275 },
276 'Transfer_Alert__c'
277 )
278 .filters(new List<String>{ 'External_Id__c IN :externalIds' })
279 .build()
280 // SYSTEM_MODE: post-upsert re-query in trigger context — same rule-4 rationale.
281 .execute(
282 new Map<String, Object>{ 'externalIds' => externalIds },
283 AccessLevel.SYSTEM_MODE
284 );
285 S_TransferAlertNotifier.notifyReceivingUsers(alertsForNotify);
286 } catch (Exception e) {
287 Logger.getInstance()
288 .error('TransferGroupTriggerService.publishHotTransferAlerts failed', e);
289 Logger.getInstance().flush();
290 }
291 }
292

97294 * ⚠️ UNVALIDATED — DO NOT BUILD ON (BMS-4665). Gulf (the target customer) has NOT confirmed
98295 * requirements for this experience, and it introduces a validation gate where none previously

docs/product/wms/internal-transfer-alerts/release-notes-snippet.md new +28 -0

1## Internal Transfer Alerts
2
3When a warehouse dispatches an internal transfer, receiving warehouse teams are now automatically alerted to any inbound inventory that is close to its code date or flagged as a hot/priority transfer — so dock staff can prioritise unloading and avoid accepting product they can't sell through.
4
5### Close-to-Code alerts
6
7When a `Transfer_Group__c` transitions **New → In Progress**, Ohanafy evaluates every transfer line against the receiving warehouse's existing close-to-code thresholds (`CTC_Threshold__mdt`). Lines whose FEFO lot falls inside the receiving warehouse's Critical, Act, or Monitor band generate a `Transfer_Alert__c` record — one per flagged transfer line — carrying the item, lot, quantity, days to code, and urgency band.
8
9A platform notification is also sent to all users assigned the **WMS Inbound Transfer Alert Receiver** permission set, so receiving staff are notified in real time on desktop and mobile.
10
11### Hot Transfer flag
12
13Dispatch managers can mark any `Transfer_Group__c` as a hot/priority transfer by toggling **Is Hot Transfer** on the Transfer Group record and selecting a priority reason (Promotion, Emergency Stock, Customer Commitment, or Other). A `Transfer_Alert__c` of type `Hot Transfer` is immediately created for the receiving warehouse and a platform notification is dispatched.
14
15### Acknowledgment
16
17Each alert has an **Acknowledge** button visible to receiving staff. Acknowledging stamps the alert with the acknowledging user's name and timestamp and changes its status from New to Acknowledged. The banner and queue continue to show recently-acknowledged alerts (last 48 hours) so supervisors retain context.
18
19### New UI surfaces
20
21**Transfer Group record page — Alert Banner:** A new `transferAlertBanner` component appears in the header of every Transfer Group record page. It shows:
22
23- Hot Transfer alert in a danger-colored banner with the priority reason.
24- Close-to-Code alerts with type and urgency-band pills, item name, days to code, case quantity, and lot number.
25- A "Partial — X of Y items flagged" label when only some of the transfer's line items are close to code.
26- Per-alert Acknowledge button; acknowledged state shows who acknowledged and when.
27
28**Warehouse Layout home page — Inbound Alert Queue:** A new `transferAlertQueue` component on the Warehouse Layout app page shows all inbound alerts for a configured warehouse location. Alerts sort hot-first, then by urgency band (Critical → Act → Monitor), then by detection time. Alerts open longer than 4 hours without acknowledgment show an **Overdue** badge.

129 <h1>Internal Transfer Alerts — Setup</h1>
130 <p class="subtitle">For Salesforce Admins configuring the feature</p>
131
132 <nav>
133 <h4>Contents</h4>
134 <ol>
135 <li><a href="#permission-set">Assign the receiving-staff permission set</a></li>
136 <li><a href="#queue">Add the Alert Queue to the Warehouse Layout page</a></li>
137 <li><a href="#thresholds">Configure close-to-code thresholds (optional)</a></li>
138 <li><a href="#limits">Limitations</a></li>
139 </ol>
140 </nav>
141
142 <h2 id="permission-set">
143 <span class="step-num">1</span> Assign the receiving-staff permission set
144 </h2>
145 <p>
146 Users who should receive platform notifications must be assigned the
147 <strong>WMS Inbound Transfer Alert Receiver</strong> permission set.
148 </p>
149 <ol>
150 <li>
151 Go to
152 <strong>Setup → Permission Sets → WMS Inbound Transfer Alert Receiver</strong>.
153 </li>
154 <li>Click <strong>Manage Assignments → Add Assignments</strong>.</li>
155 <li>
156 Select every user in a receiving role: dock supervisors, warehouse managers,
157 inventory coordinators.
158 </li>
159 <li>Click <strong>Assign</strong>.</li>
160 </ol>
161 <div class="note">
162 Users without this assignment will not receive platform notifications. The alert
163 banner on the Transfer Group record page is visible to anyone with object access to
164 <code>Transfer_Alert__c</code> regardless of this assignment.
165 </div>
166
167 <h2 id="queue">
168 <span class="step-num">2</span> Add the Alert Queue to the Warehouse Layout app page
169 </h2>
170 <p>
171 The <code>transferAlertQueue</code> component must be placed on the
172 <strong>Warehouse Layout</strong> app page and configured with the warehouse's
173 Location record ID.
174 </p>
175 <ol>
176 <li>
177 Open <strong>Setup → Lightning App Builder → Warehouse Layout → Edit</strong>.
178 </li>
179 <li>
180 Drag the <strong>Transfer Alert Queue</strong> component to the desired region
181 (recommended: above the warehouse visualization).
182 </li>
183 <li>
184 In the component's property panel, set <strong>Location Id</strong> to the
185 Salesforce record ID of the <code>Location__c</code> record that represents the
186 warehouse — the same record used as <code>Receiving_Location__c</code> on
187 inbound transfers.
188 </li>
189 <li>Click <strong>Save</strong> and then <strong>Activate</strong>.</li>
190 </ol>
191 <div class="note">
192 If multiple warehouses share one app page via tabs, add one
193 <code>transferAlertQueue</code> component per tab and configure each with its own
194 location ID.
195 </div>
196
197 <h2 id="thresholds">
198 <span class="step-num">3</span> Configure close-to-code thresholds (optional)
199 </h2>
200 <p>
201 Close-to-Code alerts use the receiving warehouse's existing
202 <code>CTC_Threshold__mdt</code> rows — the same configuration that drives the CTC
203 Suggestion Engine. If a warehouse has no threshold rows configured, no CTC alerts
204 will fire for inbound transfers to that warehouse.
205 </p>
206 <p>
207 See the
208 <a href="../ctc-suggestion-engine/">CTC Suggestion Engine setup guide</a> for
209 threshold configuration instructions.
210 </p>
211
212 <h2 id="limits">Limitations</h2>
213 <ul>
214 <li>
215 Only transfer lines with a tracked, sellable lot at the origin location generate
216 CTC alerts. Items without lot tracking or with no qualifying on-hand lot produce
217 no alert.
218 </li>
219 <li>
220 Hot Transfer alerts are created when <strong>Is Hot Transfer</strong> is toggled
221 on. Toggling it back off does not retract the existing alert; it remains in the
222 queue (with Acknowledged status) for 48 hours.
223 </li>
224 <li>
225 The Overdue badge threshold is fixed at 4 hours in the v1 release. Per-warehouse
226 escalation hours are planned for a future iteration.
227 </li>
228 </ul>
229 </div>

1# Internal Transfer Alerts — Setup
2
3## What it does
4
5Internal Transfer Alerts notify receiving warehouse staff whenever an inbound transfer carries close-to-code or hot-priority inventory. Setup involves two steps: assigning the receiving-staff permission set, and configuring the warehouse queue component on the Warehouse Layout app page.
6
7## Setup
8
9### 1. Assign the receiving-staff permission set
10
11Users who should receive platform notifications and appear in the alert queue must be assigned the **WMS Inbound Transfer Alert Receiver** permission set.
12
13In Setup → Permission Sets → **WMS Inbound Transfer Alert Receiver** → Manage Assignments → Add Assignments, select every user in a receiving role (dock supervisors, warehouse managers, inventory coordinators).
14
15Users without this assignment will not receive platform notifications and will not be resolved as recipients for new alerts. The banner on the Transfer Group record page is visible to anyone with object-level access to `Transfer_Alert__c` regardless of this permission set.
16
17### 2. Add the Alert Queue to the Warehouse Layout app page
18
19The `transferAlertQueue` component must be placed on the **Warehouse Layout** app page and configured with the warehouse's Location record ID.
20
211. Open **App Builder** for the Warehouse Layout page (Setup → Lightning App Builder → Warehouse Layout → Edit).
222. Drag the **Transfer Alert Queue** component to the desired region (recommended: above the warehouse visualization).
233. In the component's property panel, set **Location Id** to the Salesforce record ID of the `Location__c` record that represents the warehouse (the same record used as `Receiving_Location__c` on inbound transfers).
244. Save and activate.
25
26Repeat for each warehouse that should have its own queue view. If multiple warehouses share one app page (tabbed layout), place one `transferAlertQueue` component per tab, each configured with its own location ID.
27
28### 3. (Optional) Configure close-to-code thresholds
29
30Close-to-Code alerts use the receiving warehouse's existing `CTC_Threshold__mdt` rows — the same configuration that drives the CTC Suggestion Engine. If a warehouse has no threshold rows, no CTC alerts will fire for inbound transfers to that warehouse. See [CTC Suggestion Engine setup](../ctc-suggestion-engine/) for threshold configuration.
31
32## Limitations
33
34- Only transfer lines with a tracked, sellable lot at the origin location generate CTC alerts. Items without lot tracking or with no qualifying on-hand lot produce no alert.
35- Hot Transfer alerts are created immediately when `Is Hot Transfer` is toggled on. Toggling the flag off does not retract the existing alert; it remains visible (with Acknowledged status) in the queue for 48 hours.
36- The Overdue badge threshold (4 hours) is fixed in the v1 release. Per-warehouse escalation hours are planned for a future iteration.

1# Internal Transfer Alerts — Usage
2
3## What it does
4
5Receiving warehouse staff see alerts on two surfaces: the Transfer Group record page (for drill-in review of a specific transfer) and the Warehouse Layout home page (for a real-time queue of all pending inbound alerts across the warehouse).
6
7## Transfer Group record page — Alert Banner
8
9When you open a Transfer Group that has been dispatched (status In Progress), the **transferAlertBanner** component appears at the top of the page if any alerts exist.
10
11### Reading the banner
12
13Each alert row shows:
14
15| Element | Meaning |
16| ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
17| **HOT TRANSFER** pill (red) | Transfer was flagged as hot/priority by the sending manager |
18| **CTC** pill (orange/red) | One or more transfer lines are close to code |
19| Urgency band pill | **Critical** / **Act** / **Monitor** — severity against the receiving warehouse's threshold |
20| Item name | The SKU flagged on that transfer line |
21| Days to code | Calendar days remaining before the lot's code date |
22| Cases | Quantity on that transfer line |
23| Priority Reason | Shown on Hot Transfer alerts (e.g. "Promotion", "Emergency Stock") |
24| **Partial — X of Y items flagged** | Only some of the transfer's line items are close to code; X is the CTC-flagged item count, Y is the total item count on the transfer |
25
26### Acknowledging an alert
27
28Click **Acknowledge** on any alert row to mark it as reviewed. The button is replaced with "Acknowledged by [Your Name] at [time]". Acknowledgment does not block receiving or change any inventory — it is a coordination signal for supervisors monitoring the queue.
29
30## Warehouse Layout home page — Inbound Alert Queue
31
32The **transferAlertQueue** component shows all pending (New) and recently-acknowledged (last 48 hours) alerts for the configured warehouse location.
33
34### Sort order
35
36Alerts sort in this priority:
37
381. Hot Transfer alerts first
392. Close-to-Code by urgency: Critical → Act → Monitor
403. Within each group: oldest detection time first
41
42### Overdue badge
43
44Any New (unacknowledged) alert older than 4 hours shows an **Overdue** badge in orange. This signals that the transfer may have already arrived at the dock and the alert has not been reviewed.
45
46### Acknowledging from the queue
47
48Each alert row has an **Acknowledge** button. Clicking it marks the alert as acknowledged and refreshes the queue immediately. Acknowledged alerts remain visible in a grayed-out state for 48 hours, then drop off the queue.
49
50## Platform notifications
51
52Users assigned the **WMS Inbound Transfer Alert Receiver** permission set receive a platform notification (bell icon + mobile push if enabled) each time a new alert is created. The notification links directly to the `Transfer_Alert__c` record. Notifications are sent per-alert at the time the alert is created, not batched.
53
54## Flagging a Hot Transfer (sending manager)
55
56On any Transfer Group record in Draft or In Progress status, toggle **Is Hot Transfer** to `true` and select a **Priority Reason**. An optional **Hot Transfer Notes** field is available for additional context. The alert is created and notifications are sent immediately on save; the receiving warehouse queue updates in real time.

test-automation/support/subsets.ts new +61 -0
