1// Subset registration for multi-worker Playwright runs.
2//
3// A *subset* is a named group of spec files that share mutable Salesforce
4// state and therefore must run serially even when the suite runs with
5// multiple workers. Two files in the same subset can never execute
6// concurrently; files in different subsets parallelize freely.
7//
8// v1 subsets are the test folders — each folder is the shared-state boundary
9// observed in the suite today:
10//
11// | Subset | Shared mutable state serialized by the lock |
12// | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
13// | `oms` | Persistent draft Invoice__c on seed:acct-customer-0, FLP |
14// | | pricing, market-restriction switchboard, holiday templates |
15// | `wms` | Picking_Configuration__c, pick locations/assignments, |
16// | | warehouse layout, replenishment + slotting state |
17// | `pltfm` | Retail_Inventory__c quantities on seeded W1 locations |
18// | `theming` | Org-wide theme CMDTs, Theme_Palette__c, org branding |
19//
20// When the worker count grows past 2, split a folder into finer subsets
21// (e.g. `oms-invoice` vs `oms-dispatch`) by passing a finer name here —
22// but only after auditing that the split groups share NO org state.
23//
24// Usage — first statement after the imports in every spec file:
25//
26// import { test, expect } from "../../fixtures/salesforce.fixture";
27// import { registerSubsetLock } from "../../support/subsets";
28//
29// registerSubsetLock(test, "oms");
30//
31// Placement matters: registering at the top level (outside any describe)
32// guarantees the lock's beforeAll runs before describe-scoped beforeAll
33// hooks (which may mutate org state), and its afterAll runs after theirs.
34
35import type { test as sfTest } from "@playwright/test";
36
37import { acquireLock, releaseLock } from "./workerLock";
38
39/** The known v1 subsets. Keep in sync with the table above. */
40export const SUBSETS = ["oms", "wms", "pltfm", "rex", "theming"] as const;
41export type Subset = (typeof SUBSETS)[number] | (string & {});
42
43/**
44 * Serializes every test in the calling spec file against all other spec
45 * files registered under the same subset name, across all Playwright
46 * workers. Call once per spec file, at the top level, before any hooks or
47 * describe blocks.
48 */
49export function registerSubsetLock(test: typeof sfTest, subset: Subset): void {
50 test.beforeAll(async ({}, testInfo) => {
51 // The default hook timeout (= test timeout, 120s) is far too short:
52 // waiting here while the other worker finishes a whole same-subset
53 // spec file is normal, not a hang. acquireLock enforces its own
54 // 20-minute ceiling with a diagnostic error.
55 testInfo.setTimeout(22 * 60 * 1000);
56 await acquireLock(`subset-${subset}`);
57 });
58 test.afterAll(async () => {
59 releaseLock(`subset-${subset}`);
60 });
61}
test-automation/support/workerLock.ts new +123 -0
@@ -0 +1 @@
1// Cross-worker named mutex (BMS multi-worker Playwright support).
2//
3// Playwright serializes tests *within* a spec file on one worker, but with
4// `workers: 2` two different spec files run concurrently in separate OS
5// processes. Specs that share mutable org state (the persistent draft
6// invoice, org-wide theming CMDTs, picking configuration, ...) must never
7// co-run — this module provides the mutual exclusion primitive.
8//
9// Implementation: an atomic create-exclusive lock file under
10// test-automation/.locks/<name>.lock — `writeFileSync(..., { flag: "wx" })`
11// claims the lock and records holder metadata in ONE syscall, so waiters can
12// never observe a claimed-but-metadata-less lock (a directory+meta-file
13// scheme has exactly that race). All workers (local runs and the CI runner)
14// live on one machine, so a filesystem lock is sufficient.
15//
16// Safety properties:
17// - Atomic acquire: open(2) with O_CREAT|O_EXCL either creates the file or
18// throws EEXIST.
19// - Reentrant per process: if the holder pid is this process (e.g. an
20// afterAll was skipped after a crash mid-file), acquire succeeds instead
21// of self-deadlocking.
22// - Stale takeover: if the holder pid is dead (worker crashed / was killed
23// before its afterAll ran) or the lock exceeds MAX_HOLD_MS, the lock is
24// reclaimed.
25
26import fs from "fs";
27import path from "path";
28
29const LOCK_ROOT = path.join(__dirname, "..", ".locks");
30
31/** How long acquire() waits before giving up. Generous: a whole spec file of
32 * the same subset may need to finish on the other worker first. */
33const ACQUIRE_TIMEOUT_MS = 20 * 60 * 1000;
34const POLL_MS = 750;
35/** A lock older than this is presumed leaked even if the pid is alive. */
36const MAX_HOLD_MS = 25 * 60 * 1000;
37
38interface LockMeta {
39 pid: number;
40 acquiredAt: number;
41 name: string;
42}
43
44function lockPath(name: string): string {
45 return path.join(LOCK_ROOT, `${name.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, "_")}.lock`);
46}
47
48function pidAlive(pid: number): boolean {
49 try {
50 process.kill(pid, 0);
51 return true;
52 } catch {
53 return false;
54 }
55}
56
57function readMeta(file: string): LockMeta | null {
58 try {
59 return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf-8")) as LockMeta;
60 } catch {
61 return null;
62 }
63}
64
65function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
66 return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
67}
68
69/**
70 * Acquires the named lock, waiting (polling) while another live process holds
71 * it. Throws after ACQUIRE_TIMEOUT_MS with a diagnostic naming the holder.
72 */
73export async function acquireLock(name: string): Promise<void> {
74 fs.mkdirSync(LOCK_ROOT, { recursive: true });
75 const file = lockPath(name);
76 const deadline = Date.now() + ACQUIRE_TIMEOUT_MS;
77
78 for (;;) {
79 const meta: LockMeta = { pid: process.pid, acquiredAt: Date.now(), name };
80 try {
81 fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(meta), { flag: "wx" });
82 return;
83 } catch (err) {
84 if ((err as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw err;
85 }
86
87 const holder = readMeta(file);
88 const stale =
89 !holder || // deleted between our EEXIST and the read — retry
90 holder.pid === process.pid || // reentrant: we already hold it
91 !pidAlive(holder.pid) || // holder process died
92 Date.now() - holder.acquiredAt > MAX_HOLD_MS;
93
94 if (stale) {
95 // Two waiters may reclaim concurrently — unlink can race, ignore.
96 try {
97 fs.rmSync(file, { force: true });
98 } catch {
99 /* lost the reclaim race; loop and retry */
100 }
101 continue;
102 }
103
104 if (Date.now() > deadline) {
105 throw new Error(
106 `Timed out after ${ACQUIRE_TIMEOUT_MS / 60000}m waiting for lock '${name}' ` +
107 `held by pid ${holder.pid} since ${new Date(holder.acquiredAt).toISOString()}. ` +
108 `If no other Playwright run is active, delete ${file} and retry.`
109 );
110 }
111 await sleep(POLL_MS);
112 }
113}
114
115/** Releases the named lock if this process holds it (no-op otherwise). */
116export function releaseLock(name: string): void {
117 const file = lockPath(name);
118 const meta = readMeta(file);
119 if (meta && meta.pid !== process.pid) {
120 return; // someone else reclaimed it (stale takeover) — leave it alone
121 }

8/**
9 * Seeds two locations, two Transfer__c line items, one Transfer Group, and three
10 * Transfer_Alert__c records:
11 * - ctcAlert (Close to Code / Critical / New, linked to item 1) — render + partial-label test
12 * - hotAlert (Hot Transfer / New) — hot-transfer render test
13 * - ackAlert (Close to Code / Act / New) — dedicated acknowledge test

15 * Two Transfer__c lines ensure getTransferLineCount() = 2 so ctcItemCount (1) < total (2),
16 * which makes showPartialLabel = true for the AC4 assertion.

18 * Seeded once at module-load (synchronous runSfApex) so the org is stable for all tests.

44);

48// This makes getTransferLineCount()=2 and ctcItemCount=1 → showPartialLabel=true (AC4).

57);

60 Origin_Location__c = origin.Id,
61 New_Location__c = dest.Id,
62 Transfer_Quantity_Cases__c = 3,
63 Transfer_Date__c = Date.today(),
64 Transfer_Group__c = grp.Id,
65 Is_Sellable__c = true
66);
67insert new List<Transfer__c>{ line1, line2 };
68
69Transfer_Alert__c ctcAlert = new Transfer_Alert__c(

71 Receiving_Location__c = dest.Id,
72 Origin_Location__c = origin.Id,
73 Alert_Type__c = 'Close to Code',
74 Urgency_Band__c = 'Critical',
75 Status__c = 'New',

95 Receiving_Location__c = dest.Id,

114}));
